sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\n26\tfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n27\t\n28\tGRID_CELLS = {\n29\t    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n30\t    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n31\t    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n32\t    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n33\t    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n34\t    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n35\t    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n36\t    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n37\t    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n38\t}\n39\t\n40\tMODEL_PATHS = {\n41\t    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n42\t    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n43\t}\n44\t\n45\tNUM_DEPTHS = 4\n46\tMAX_SEQ_LEN = 512\n47\tBATCH_SIZE = 16\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tclass TextDataset(Dataset):\n51\t    def __init__(self, texts, tokenizer, max_len=512):\n52\t        self.encodings = tokenizer(\n53\t            texts, max_length=max_len, truncation=True,\n54\t            padding=\"max_length\", return_tensors=\"pt\"\n55\t        )\n56\t\n57\t    def __len__(self):\n58\t        return self.encodings[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n59\t\n60\t    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n61\t        return {k: v[idx] for k, v in self.encodings.items()}\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tdef load_cell_data(data_dir, max_per_depth=5000):\n65\t    texts, labels = [], []\n66\t    for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS):\n67\t        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\"depth_{d}.jsonl\")\n68\t        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\n69\t            print(f\"  WARNING: {fpath} not found, skipping depth {d}\")\n70\t            continue\n71\t        count = 0\n72\t        with open(fpath) as f:\n73\t            for line in f:\n74\t                line = line.strip()\n75\t                if not line:\n76\t                    continue\n77\t                rec = json.loads(line)\n78\t                texts.append(rec[\"text\"])\n79\t                labels.append(d)\n80\t                count += 1\n81\t                if count >= max_per_depth:\n82\t                    break\n83\t        print(f\"  depth_{d}: {count} samples\")\n84\t    return texts, np.array(labels)\n85\t\n86\t\n87\t@torch.no_grad()\n88\tdef compute_log_likelihoods(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE):\n89\t    \"\"\"Compute per-sample mean log-likelihood (negative cross-entropy).\"\"\"\n90\t    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n91\t    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n92\t\n93\t    all_ll = []\n94\t    for batch in loader:\n95\t        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n96\t        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n97\t\n98\t        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n99\t        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]  # shift\n100\t        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]  # shift\n101\t        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\n102\t\n103\t        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n104\t        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n105\t        token_ll = token_ll * mask\n106\t\n107\t        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n108\t        mean_ll = token_ll.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n109\t        all_ll.append(mean_ll.cpu().numpy())\n110\t\n111\t    return np.concatenate(all_ll)\n112\t\n113\t\n114\t@torch.no_grad()\n115\tdef compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE):\n116\t    \"\"\"\n117\t    Compute Fast-DetectGPT score: normalized discrepancy between\n118\t    actual token log-prob and expected log-prob under model's own distribution.\n119\t    score_i = log p(x_i|x_{&lt;i}) + H(p(·|x_{&lt;i}))\n120\t    Final score = mean(score_i) / std(score_i)\n121\t    \"\"\"\n122\t    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n123\t    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n124\t\n125\t    all_scores = []\n126\t    for batch in loader:\n127\t        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n128\t        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n129\t\n130\t        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n131\t        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n132\t        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n133\t        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n134\t\n135\t        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n136\t        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n137\t\n138\t        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n139\t        entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\n140\t\n141\t        discrepancy = token_ll + entropy  # log p(x_i) - (-H) = log p(x_i) + H\n142\t        discrepancy = discrepancy * mask\n143\t\n144\t        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n145\t        mean_d = (discrepancy * mask).sum(dim=1) / lengths\n146\t\n147\t        sum_sq = ((discrepancy - mean_d.unsqueeze(1)) ** 2 * mask).sum(dim=1)\n148\t        std_d = (sum_sq / lengths.clamp(min=2)).sqrt().clamp(min=1e-8)\n149\t\n150\t        score = mean_d / std_d\n151\t        all_scores.append(score.cpu().numpy())\n152\t\n153\t    return np.concatenate(all_scores)\n154\t\n155\t\n156\tdef optimal_binning(scores, labels, n_bins=4):\n157\t    \"\"\"Cut scores into n_bins using training quantiles, return accuracy.\"\"\"\n158\t    thresholds = np.quantile(scores, [i / n_bins for i in range(1, n_bins)])\n159\t    preds = np.digitize(scores, thresholds)\n160\t    return accuracy_score(labels, preds), preds\n161\t\n162\t\n163\tdef lr_calibration_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5, seed=42):\n164\t    \"\"\"5-fold CV logistic regression on single score feature.\"\"\"\n165\t    X = scores.reshape(-1, 1)\n166\t    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n167\t    accs = []\n168\t    all_preds = np.zeros_like(labels)\n169\t\n170\t    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, labels):\n171\t        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=seed, multi_class=\"multinomial\")\n172\t        clf.fit(X[train_idx], labels[train_idx])\n173\t        preds = clf.predict(X[test_idx])\n174\t        all_preds[test_idx] = preds\n175\t        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[test_idx], preds))\n176\t\n177\t    return np.mean(accs), np.std(accs), all_preds\n178\t\n179\t\n180\tdef run_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf, tok_obs, tok_perf, device):\n181\t    \"\"\"Run all scoring methods on one cell.\"\"\"\n182\t    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n183\t    print(f\"  Processing: {cell_name}\")\n184\t    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n185\t\n186\t    texts, labels = load_cell_data(data_dir)\n187\t    if len(texts) == 0:\n188\t        return None\n189\t    n = len(texts)\n190\t    print(f\"  Total: {n} samples, depths: {np.bincount(labels)}\")\n191\t\n192\t    t0 = time.time()\n193\t    print(\"  Computing log-likelihoods (observer: gpt2-xl)...\")\n194\t    ll_obs = compute_log_likelihoods(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device)\n195\t    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n196\t\n197\t    t1 = time.time()\n198\t    print(\"  Computing log-likelihoods (performer: pythia-1.4b)...\")\n199\t    ll_perf = compute_log_likelihoods(model_perf, tok_perf, texts, device)\n200\t    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n201\t\n202\t    t2 = time.time()\n203\t    print(\"  Computing Fast-DetectGPT scores (gpt2-xl)...\")\n204\t    fdgpt_scores = compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device)\n205\t    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t2:.1f}s\")\n206\t\n207\t    # Binoculars score: CE_observer / CE_performer = (-ll_obs) / (-ll_perf)\n208\t    ce_obs = -ll_obs\n209\t    ce_perf = -ll_perf\n210\t    bino_scores = ce_obs / np.clip(ce_perf, 1e-8, None)\n211\t\n212\t    results = {\"cell\": cell_name, \"n_samples\": n}\n213\t\n214\t    # Binoculars calibration\n215\t    bino_bin_acc, _ = optimal_binning(bino_scores, labels)\n216\t    bino_lr_acc, bino_lr_std, _ = lr_calibration_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n217\t    results[\"binoculars\"] = {\n218\t        \"binning_acc\": round(bino_bin_acc, 4),\n219\t        \"lr_acc_mean\": round(bino_lr_acc, 4),\n220\t        \"lr_acc_std\": round(bino_lr_std, 4),\n221\t        \"score_stats\": {\n222\t            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n223\t                      \"std\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].std()), 4)}\n224\t            for d in lr_calibration_cv(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n231\t    results[\"fast_detectgpt\"] = {\n232\t        \"binning_acc\": round(fdgpt_bin_acc, 4),\n233\t        \"lr_acc_mean\": round(fdgpt_lr_acc, 4),\n234\t   X_combined = np.column_stack([bino_scores, fdgpt_scores])\n244\t    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n245\t    comb_accs = []\n246\t    for train_idx, test_idx in      \"hypothesis\": \"Binoculars/Fast-DetectGPT continuous scores cannot be effectively \"\n341\t                      \"calibrated to ordinal depth because they capture real-vs-synthetic \"\n342\t baselines for recursive generation depth estimation.\n\nComputes Binoculars and Fast-DetectGPT continuous scores on 3x3 grid data,\nthen calibrates to 4-class ordinal prediction via LR and optimal binning.\n\nModels used:\n  - Binoculars: gpt2-xl (observer) + pythia-1.4b (performer)\n  - Fast-DetectGPT: gpt2-xl (scoring model)\n\nUsage:\n  python scripts/detection_baselines.py --gpu 0\n  python scripts/detection_baselines.py --gpu 0 --pilot  # only pythia_nucleus\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\n\nGRID_CELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n}\n\nMODEL_PATHS = {\n    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n}\n\nNUM_DEPTHS = 4\nMAX_SEQ_LEN = 512\nBATCH_SIZE = 16\n\n\nclass TextDataset(Dataset):\n    def __init__(self, texts, tokenizer, max_len=512):\n        self.encodings = tokenizer(\n            texts, max_length=max_len, truncation=True,\n            padding=\"max_length\", return_tensors=\"pt\"\n        )\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return self.encodings[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n        return {k: v[idx] for k, v in self.encodings.items()}\n\n\ndef load_cell_data(data_dir, max_per_depth=5000):\n    texts, labels = [], []\n    for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS):\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\"depth_{d}.jsonl\")\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\n            print(f\"  WARNING: {fpath} not found, skipping depth {d}\")\n            continue\n        count = 0\n        with open(fpath) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                texts.append(rec[\"text\"])\n                labels.append(d)\n                count += 1\n                if count >= max_per_depth:\n                    break\n        print(f\"  depth_{d}: {count} samples\")\n    return texts, np.array(labels)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_log_likelihoods(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE):\n    \"\"\"Compute per-sample mean log-likelihood (negative cross-entropy).\"\"\"\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n\n    all_ll = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]  # shift\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]  # shift\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\n\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        token_ll = token_ll * mask\n\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_ll = token_ll.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_ll.append(mean_ll.cpu().numpy())\n\n    return np.concatenate(all_ll)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute Fast-DetectGPT score: normalized discrepancy between\n    actual token log-prob and expected log-prob under model's own distribution.\n    score_i = log p(x_i|x_{&lt;i}) + H(p(·|x_{&lt;i}))\n    Final score = mean(score_i) / std(score_i)\n    \"\"\"\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n\n    all_scores = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\n\n        discrepancy = token_ll + entropy  # log p(x_i) - (-H) = log p(x_i) + H\n        discrepancy = discrepancy * mask\n\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_d = (discrepancy * mask).sum(dim=1) / lengths\n\n        sum_sq = ((discrepancy - mean_d.unsqueeze(1)) ** 2 * mask).sum(dim=1)\n        std_d = (sum_sq / lengths.clamp(min=2)).sqrt().clamp(min=1e-8)\n\n        score = mean_d / std_d\n        all_scores.append(score.cpu().numpy())\n\n    return np.concatenate(all_scores)\n\n\ndef optimal_binning(scores, labels, n_bins=4):\n    \"\"\"Cut scores into n_bins using training quantiles, return accuracy.\"\"\"\n    thresholds = np.quantile(scores, [i / n_bins for i in range(1, n_bins)])\n    preds = np.digitize(scores, thresholds)\n    return accuracy_score(labels, preds), preds\n\n\ndef lr_calibration_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5, seed=42):\n    \"\"\"5-fold CV logistic regression on single score feature.\"\"\"\n    X = scores.reshape(-1, 1)\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    accs = []\n    all_preds = np.zeros_like(labels)\n\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, labels):\n        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=seed, multi_class=\"multinomial\")\n        clf.fit(X[train_idx], labels[train_idx])\n        preds = clf.predict(X[test_idx])\n        all_preds[test_idx] = preds\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[test_idx], preds))\n\n    return np.mean(accs), np.std(accs), all_preds\n\n\ndef run_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf, tok_obs, tok_perf, device):\n    \"\"\"Run all scoring methods on one cell.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Processing: {cell_name}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    texts, labels = load_cell_data(data_dir)\n    if len(texts) == 0:\n        return None\n    n = len(texts)\n    print(f\"  Total: {n} samples, depths: {np.bincount(labels)}\")\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    print(\"  Computing log-likelihoods (observer: gpt2-xl)...\")\n    ll_obs = compute_log_likelihoods(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device)\n    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n    print(\"  Computing log-likelihoods (performer: pythia-1.4b)...\")\n    ll_perf = compute_log_likelihoods(model_perf, tok_perf, texts, device)\n    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n\n    t2 = time.time()\n    print(\"  Computing Fast-DetectGPT scores (gpt2-xl)...\")\n    fdgpt_scores = compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device)\n    print(f\"    Done in {time.time()-t2:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Binoculars score: CE_observer / CE_performer = (-ll_obs) / (-ll_perf)\n    ce_obs = -ll_obs\n    ce_perf = -ll_perf\n    bino_scores = ce_obs / np.clip(ce_perf, 1e-8, None)\n\n    results = {\"cell\": cell_name, \"n_samples\": n}\n\n    # Binoculars calibration\n    bino_bin_acc, _ = optimal_binning(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_lr_acc, bino_lr_std, _ = lr_calibration_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    results[\"binoculars\"] = {\n        \"binning_acc\": round(bino_bin_acc, 4),\n        \"lr_acc_mean\": round(bino_lr_acc, 4),\n        \"lr_acc_std\": round(bino_lr_std, 4),\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                      \"std\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].std()), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Fast-DetectGPT calibration\n    fdgpt_bin_acc, _ = optimal_binning(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_lr_acc, fdgpt_lr_std, _ = lr_calibration_cv(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    results[\"fast_detectgpt\"] = {\n        \"binning_acc\": round(fdgpt_bin_acc, 4),\n        \"lr_acc_mean\": round(fdgpt_lr_acc, 4),\n        \"lr_acc_std\": round(fdgpt_lr_std, 4),\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(fdgpt_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                      \"std\": round(float(fdgpt_scores[labels == d].std()), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Combined (both scores as 2-feature input)\n    X_combined = np.column_stack([bino_scores, fdgpt_scores])\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    comb_accs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X_combined, labels):\n        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42, multi_class=\"multinomial\")\n        clf.fit(X_combined[train_idx], labels[train_idx])\n        comb_accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[test_idx], clf.predict(X_combined[test_idx])))\n    results[\"combined_lr_acc\"] = round(np.mean(comb_accs), 4)\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Results for {cell_name}:\")\n    print(f\"    Binoculars  — Binning: {bino_bin_acc:.4f}, LR: {bino_lr_acc:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"    FastDetect  — Binning: {fdgpt_bin_acc:.4f}, LR: {fdgpt_lr_acc:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"    Combined LR — {np.mean(comb_accs):.4f}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--pilot\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Only run pythia_nucleus\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=BATCH_SIZE)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"results/detection_baselines\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    global BATCH_SIZE\n    BATCH_SIZE = args.batch_size\n\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    print(\"Loading models...\")\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    tok_obs = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATHS[\"gpt2xl\"])\n    tok_obs.pad_token = tok_obs.eos_token\n    model_obs = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATHS[\"gpt2xl\"], torch_dtype=torch.float16\n    ).to(device).eval()\n    print(f\"  gpt2-xl loaded in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n    tok_perf = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATHS[\"pythia\"])\n    tok_perf.pad_token = tok_perf.eos_token\n    model_perf = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATHS[\"pythia\"], torch_dtype=torch.float16\n    ).to(device).eval()\n    print(f\"  pythia-1.4b loaded in {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n\n    cells = {\"pythia_nucleus\": GRID_CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus\"]} if args.pilot else GRID_CELLS\n    all_results = {}\n    start = time.time()\n\n    for cell_name, data_dir in cells.items():\n        result = run_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf, tok_obs, tok_perf, device)\n        if result:\n            all_results[cell_name] = result\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - start\n\n    # RF reference values from baseline_battery_9cell\n    rf_reference = {\n        \"pythia_nucleus\": 0.7813, \"pythia_temp09\": 0.7677, \"pythia_topk50\": 0.687,\n        \"olmo_nucleus\": 0.7877, \"olmo_temp09\": 0.827, \"olmo_topk50\": 0.737,\n        \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": 0.8397, \"gpt2xl_temp09\": 0.8527, \"gpt2xl_topk50\": 0.754,\n    }\n\n    # Summary table\n    print(f\"\\n\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(\"  COMPARISON TABLE: 4-class Accuracy (LR calibration)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"  {'Cell':<18} {'RF(19feat)':<12} {'Bino-LR':<12} {'FDG-LR':<12} {'Combined':<12}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*18} {'-'*12} {'-'*12} {'-'*12} {'-'*12}\")\n\n    bino_accs, fdgpt_accs, comb_accs = [], [], []\n    for cell_name in cells:\n        if cell_name not in all_results:\n            continue\n        r = all_results[cell_name]\n        rf = rf_reference.get(cell_name, 0)\n        b = r[\"binoculars\"][\"lr_acc_mean\"]\n        f = r[\"fast_detectgpt\"][\"lr_acc_mean\"]\n        c = r[\"combined_lr_acc\"]\n        print(f\"  {cell_name:<18} {rf:<12.4f} {b:<12.4f} {f:<12.4f} {c:<12.4f}\")\n        bino_accs.append(b)\n        fdgpt_accs.append(f)\n        comb_accs.append(c)\n\n    if bino_accs:\n        rf_vals = [rf_reference[c] for c in cells if c in all_results]\n        print(f\"  {'-'*18} {'-'*12} {'-'*12} {'-'*12} {'-'*12}\")\n        print(f\"  {'MEAN':<18} {np.mean(rf_vals):<12.4f} {np.mean(bino_accs):<12.4f} \"\n              f\"{np.mean(fdgpt_accs):<12.4f} {np.mean(comb_accs):<12.4f}\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"experiment\": \"detection_baselines_001\",\n        \"plan_id\": \"plan_018\",\n        \"hypothesis\": \"Binoculars/Fast-DetectGPT continuous scores cannot be effectively \"\n                      \"calibrated to ordinal depth because they capture real-vs-synthetic \"\n                      \"boundary rather than depth gradient; calibrated 4-class acc < 45%\",\n        \"models\": {\n            \"observer\": \"gpt2-xl (local)\",\n            \"performer\": \"pythia-1.4b (local)\",\n            \"fast_detectgpt_scorer\": \"gpt2-xl (local)\"\n        },\n        \"per_cell\": all_results,\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"mean_binoculars_lr_acc\": round(np.mean(bino_accs), 4) if bino_accs else None,\n            \"mean_fast_detectgpt_lr_acc\": round(np.mean(fdgpt_accs), 4) if fdgpt_accs else None,\n            \"mean_combined_lr_acc\": round(np.mean(comb_accs), 4) if comb_accs else None,\n            \"mean_rf_reference\": round(np.mean(rf_vals), 4) if bino_accs else None,\n        },\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012J1ghEmHrXtsJ3oEm88WBh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Rb89UCzVuUFUYGGGaoP7P3","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py","content":"\"\"\"\nOrdinal depth resolution analysis for Binoculars / Fast-DetectGPT baselines.\n\nExtends detection_baselines.py with:\n  - Pairwise AUC (binary RF on adjacent depth pairs)\n  - Monotonicity (Spearman rho)\n  - Isotonic regression calibration\n  - d0-binary accuracy\n  - Per-depth score distributions\n\nUsage:\n  python scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py --gpu 0 --pilot\n  python scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py --gpu 0  # all 9 cells\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.isotonic import IsotonicRegression\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, roc_auc_score\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\n\nGRID_CELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n}\n\nMODEL_PATHS = {\n    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n}\n\nNUM_DEPTHS = 4\nMAX_SEQ_LEN = 512\nDEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE = 16\n\nRF_REFERENCE = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\":  {\"acc\": 0.787, \"d0v1\": 0.999, \"d1v2\": 0.895, \"d2v3\": 0.760},\n    \"pythia_temp09\":   {\"acc\": 0.768},\n    \"pythia_topk50\":   {\"acc\": 0.689},\n    \"olmo_nucleus\":    {\"acc\": 0.789},\n    \"olmo_temp09\":     {\"acc\": 0.823},\n    \"olmo_topk50\":     {\"acc\": 0.744},\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\":  {\"acc\": 0.843},\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\":   {\"acc\": 0.859},\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\":   {\"acc\": 0.758},\n}\n\n\nclass TextDataset(Dataset):\n    def __init__(self, texts, tokenizer, max_len=512):\n        self.encodings = tokenizer(\n            texts, max_length=max_len, truncation=True,\n            padding=\"max_length\", return_tensors=\"pt\"\n        )\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return self.encodings[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n        return {k: v[idx] for k, v in self.encodings.items()}\n\n\ndef load_cell_data(data_dir, max_per_depth=5000):\n    texts, labels = [], []\n    for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS):\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\"depth_{d}.jsonl\")\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\n            print(f\"  WARNING: {fpath} not found, skipping depth {d}\")\n            continue\n        count = 0\n        with open(fpath) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                texts.append(rec[\"text\"])\n                labels.append(d)\n                count += 1\n                if count >= max_per_depth:\n                    break\n        print(f\"  depth_{d}: {count} samples\")\n    return texts, np.array(labels)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_log_likelihoods(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n    all_ll = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        token_ll = token_ll * mask\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_ll = token_ll.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_ll.append(mean_ll.cpu().numpy())\n    return np.concatenate(all_ll)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n    all_scores = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\n        discrepancy = token_ll + entropy\n        discrepancy = discrepancy * mask\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_d = (discrepancy * mask).sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        sum_sq = ((discrepancy - mean_d.unsqueeze(1)) ** 2 * mask).sum(dim=1)\n        std_d = (sum_sq / lengths.clamp(min=2)).sqrt().clamp(min=1e-8)\n        score = mean_d / std_d\n        all_scores.append(score.cpu().numpy())\n    return np.concatenate(all_scores)\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc(scores, labels, pair):\n    d_lo, d_hi = pair\n    mask = (labels == d_lo) | (labels == d_hi)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    X = scores[mask].reshape(-1, 1)\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_hi).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n        return None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    aucs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n        rf.fit(X[tr], y[tr])\n        prob = rf.predict_proba(X[te])[:, 1]\n        aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[te], prob))\n    return round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc_combined(bino_scores, fdgpt_scores, labels, pair):\n    d_lo, d_hi = pair\n    mask = (labels == d_lo) | (labels == d_hi)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    X = np.column_stack([bino_scores[mask], fdgpt_scores[mask]])\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_hi).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n        return None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    aucs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n        rf.fit(X[tr], y[tr])\n        prob = rf.predict_proba(X[te])[:, 1]\n        aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[te], prob))\n    return round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef compute_monotonicity(scores, labels):\n    rho, pval = spearmanr(scores, labels)\n    return round(rho, 4), round(pval, 6)\n\n\ndef isotonic_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(scores, labels):\n        iso = IsotonicRegression(y_min=0, y_max=NUM_DEPTHS - 1, out_of_bounds=\"clip\")\n        iso.fit(scores[tr], labels[tr].astype(float))\n        preds = np.round(iso.predict(scores[te])).astype(int)\n        preds = np.clip(preds, 0, NUM_DEPTHS - 1)\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[te], preds))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.std(accs), 4)\n\n\ndef d0_binary_accuracy(scores, labels):\n    binary = (labels >= 1).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(binary)) < 2:\n        return None, None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs, aucs = [], []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(scores, binary):\n        thresh = np.median(scores[tr][labels[tr] == 0])\n        higher_is_gen = np.mean(scores[tr][labels[tr] >= 1]) > np.mean(scores[tr][labels[tr] == 0])\n        if higher_is_gen:\n            preds = (scores[te] > thresh).astype(int)\n        else:\n            preds = (scores[te] < thresh).astype(int)\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(binary[te], preds))\n        if higher_is_gen:\n            aucs.append(roc_auc_score(binary[te], scores[te]))\n        else:\n            aucs.append(roc_auc_score(binary[te], -scores[te]))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef lr_4class_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5):\n    X = scores.reshape(-1, 1) if scores.ndim == 1 else scores\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, labels):\n        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42, multi_class=\"multinomial\")\n        clf.fit(X[tr], labels[tr])\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[te], clf.predict(X[te])))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.std(accs), 4)\n\n\ndef analyze_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf, tok_obs, tok_perf,\n                 device, batch_size, scores_dir):\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  {cell_name}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    texts, labels = load_cell_data(data_dir)\n    if len(texts) == 0:\n        return None\n    n = len(texts)\n    print(f\"  Total: {n} samples, depths: {np.bincount(labels)}\")\n\n    cache_path = os.path.join(scores_dir, f\"{cell_name}_scores.npz\")\n    if os.path.exists(cache_path):\n        print(f\"  Loading cached scores from {cache_path}\")\n        cached = np.load(cache_path)\n        bino_scores = cached[\"bino\"]\n        fdgpt_scores = cached[\"fdgpt\"]\n    else:\n        t0 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing LL (observer: gpt2-xl)...\")\n        ll_obs = compute_log_likelihoods(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n        t1 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing LL (performer: pythia-1.4b)...\")\n        ll_perf = compute_log_likelihoods(model_perf, tok_perf, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n\n        t2 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing Fast-DetectGPT scores...\")\n        fdgpt_scores = compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t2:.1f}s\")\n\n        bino_scores = (-ll_obs) / np.clip(-ll_perf, 1e-8, None)\n\n        np.savez(cache_path, bino=bino_scores, fdgpt=fdgpt_scores, labels=labels)\n        print(f\"  Scores cached to {cache_path}\")\n\n    result = {\"cell\": cell_name, \"n_samples\": n}\n    pairs = [(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)]\n    pair_names = [\"d0v1\", \"d1v2\", \"d2v3\"]\n\n    # --- Binoculars ---\n    bino_lr_acc, bino_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_iso_acc, bino_iso_std = isotonic_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_rho, bino_rho_p = compute_monotonicity(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_d0_acc, bino_d0_auc = d0_binary_accuracy(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        bino_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc(bino_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"binoculars\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": bino_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": bino_lr_std,\n        \"isotonic_acc\": bino_iso_acc,\n        \"isotonic_std\": bino_iso_std,\n        \"spearman_rho\": bino_rho,\n        \"spearman_p\": bino_rho_p,\n        \"d0_binary_acc\": bino_d0_acc,\n        \"d0_binary_auc\": bino_d0_auc,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": bino_pairwise,\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                      \"std\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].std()), 4),\n                      \"median\": round(float(np.median(bino_scores[labels == d])), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # --- Fast-DetectGPT ---\n    fdgpt_lr_acc, fdgpt_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_iso_acc, fdgpt_iso_std = isotonic_cv(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_rho, fdgpt_rho_p = compute_monotonicity(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_d0_acc, fdgpt_d0_auc = d0_binary_accuracy(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        fdgpt_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc(fdgpt_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"fast_detectgpt\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": fdgpt_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": fdgpt_lr_std,\n        \"isotonic_acc\": fdgpt_iso_acc,\n        \"isotonic_std\": fdgpt_iso_std,\n        \"spearman_rho\": fdgpt_rho,\n        \"spearman_p\": fdgpt_rho_p,\n        \"d0_binary_acc\": fdgpt_d0_acc,\n        \"d0_binary_auc\": fdgpt_d0_auc,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": fdgpt_pairwise,\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(fdgpt_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                      \"std\": round(float(fdgpt_scores[labels == d].std()), 4),\n                      \"median\": round(float(np.median(fdgpt_scores[labels == d])), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # --- Combined (2-feature) ---\n    X_comb = np.column_stack([bino_scores, fdgpt_scores])\n    comb_lr_acc, comb_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(X_comb, labels)\n    comb_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        comb_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc_combined(bino_scores, fdgpt_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"combined\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": comb_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": comb_lr_std,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": comb_pairwise,\n    }\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(f\"\\n  {cell_name} results:\")\n    print(f\"    Binoculars  LR-4cls: {bino_lr_acc:.4f}  Iso: {bino_iso_acc:.4f}  \"\n          f\"rho: {bino_rho:.4f}  d0-bin: {bino_d0_acc}\")\n    print(f\"    FastDetGPT  LR-4cls: {fdgpt_lr_acc:.4f}  Iso: {fdgpt_iso_acc:.4f}  \"\n          f\"rho: {fdgpt_rho:.4f}  d0-bin: {fdgpt_d0_acc}\")\n    print(f\"    Combined    LR-4cls: {comb_lr_acc:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (Bino):  d0v1={bino_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n          f\"d1v2={bino_pairwise['d1v2']}  d2v3={bino_pairwise['d2v3']}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (FDGPT): d0v1={fdgpt_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n          f\"d1v2={fdgpt_pairwise['d1v2']}  d2v3={fdgpt_pairwise['d2v3']}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (Comb):  d0v1={comb_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n          f\"d1v2={comb_pairwise['d1v2']}  d2v3={comb_pairwise['d2v3']}\")\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef print_comparison_table(all_results, cells):\n    print(f\"\\n\\n{'='*110}\")\n    print(\"  COMPARISON TABLE\")\n    print(f\"{'='*110}\")\n\n    header = (f\"  {'Cell':<16} {'Method':<14} {'4cls-Acc':<10} {'d0v1':<8} \"\n              f\"{'d1v2':<8} {'d2v3':<8} {'Spearman':<10} {'d0-bin':<8}\")\n    print(header)\n    print(f\"  {'-'*106}\")\n\n    for cell_name in cells:\n        if cell_name not in all_results:\n            continue\n        r = all_results[cell_name]\n        ref = RF_REFERENCE.get(cell_name, {})\n\n        # RF reference\n        rf_acc = ref.get(\"acc\", \"\")\n        rf_d0v1 = ref.get(\"d0v1\", \"\")\n        rf_d1v2 = ref.get(\"d1v2\", \"\")\n        rf_d2v3 = ref.get(\"d2v3\", \"\")\n        print(f\"  {cell_name:<16} {'RF-19feat':<14} {rf_acc:<10} \"\n              f\"{rf_d0v1:<8} {rf_d1v2:<8} {rf_d2v3:<8} {'-1.0':<10} {'—':<8}\")\n\n        # Binoculars\n        b = r[\"binoculars\"]\n        print(f\"  {'':<16} {'Bino-LR':<14} {b['lr_4class_acc']:<10.4f} \"\n              f\"{b['pairwise_auc']['d0v1']:<8} {b['pairwise_auc']['d1v2']:<8} \"\n              f\"{b['pairwise_auc']['d2v3']:<8} {f['lr_4class_acc']:<10.4f} \"\n              f\"{f['pairwise_auc']['d0v1']:<8} {f['pairwise_auc']['d1v2']:<8} \"\n              f\"{f['pairwise_auc']['d2v3']:<8} {f['spearman_rho']:<10.4f} \"\n              f\"{f['d0_binary_acc']:<8}\")\n\n        # Combined\n        c = r[\"combined\"]\n        print(f\"  {'':<16} {'Combined':<14} {c['lr_4class_acc']:<10.4f} \"\n              f\"{c['pairwise_auc']['d0v1']:<8} {c['pairwise_auc']['d1v2']:<8} \"\n              f\"{c['pairwise_auc']['d2v3']:<8} {'—':<10} {'—':<8}\")\n\n        print(f\"  {'-'*106}\")\n\n    # Averages\n    bino_accs = [all_results[c][\"binoculars\"][\"lr_4class_acc\"] for c in cells if c in all_results]\n    fdgpt_accs = [all_results[c][\"fast_detectgpt\"][\"lr_4class_acc\"] for c in cells if c in all_results]\n    comb_accs = [all_results[c][\"combined\"][\"lr_4class_acc\"] for c in cells if c in all_results]\n    rf_accs = [RF_REFERENCE[c][\"acc\"] for c in cells if c in all_results]\n\n    if bino_accs:\n        print(f\"\\n  Mean 4-class Acc:  RF={np.mean(rf_accs):.4f}  \"\n              f\"Bino={np.mean(bino_accs):.4f}  FDGPT={np.mean(fdgpt_accs):.4f}  \"\n              f\"Combined={np.mean(comb_accs):.4f}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--pilot\", action=\"store_true\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"results/detection_baselines_ordinal\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    scores_dir = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"scores\")\n    os.makedirs(scores_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    print(\"Loading models...\")\n    t0 = time.time()\n    tok_obs = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATHS[\"gpt2xl\"])\n    tok_obs.pad_token = tok_obs.eos_token\n    model_obs = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATHS[\"gpt2xl\"], torch_dtype=torch.float16\n    ).to(device).eval()\n    print(f\"  gpt2-xl loaded ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)\")\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n    tok_perf = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATHS[\"pythia\"])\n    tok_perf.pad_token = tok_perf.eos_token\n    model_perf = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATHS[\"pythia\"], torch_dtype=torch.float16\n    ).to(device).eval()\n    print(f\"  pythia-1.4b loaded ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)\")\n\n    cells = {\"pythia_nucleus\": GRID_CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus\"]} if args.pilot else GRID_CELLS\n    all_results = {}\n    start = time.time()\n\n    for cell_name, data_dir in cells.items():\n        r = analyze_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf,\n                         tok_obs, tok_perf, device, args.batch_size, scores_dir)\n        if r:\n            all_results[cell_name] = r\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - start\n    print_comparison_table(all_results, cells)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"experiment\": \"detection_baselines_ordinal\",\n        \"plan_id\": \"plan_018\",\n depth resolution analysis for Binoculars / Fast-DetectGPT baselines.\n\nExtends detection_baselines.py with:\n  - Pairwise AUC (binary RF on adjacent depth pairs)\n  - Monotonicity (Spearman rho)\n  - Isotonic regression calibration\n  - d0-binary accuracy\n  - Per-depth score distributions\n\nUsage:\n  python scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py --gpu 0 --pilot\n  python scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py --gpu 0  # all 9 cells\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.isotonic import IsotonicRegression\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, roc_auc_score\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\n\nGRID_CELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n}\n\nMODEL_PATHS = {\n    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n}\n\nNUM_DEPTHS = 4\nMAX_SEQ_LEN = 512\nDEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE = 16\n\nRF_REFERENCE = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\":  {\"acc\": 0.787, \"d0v1\": 0.999, \"d1v2\": 0.895, \"d2v3\": 0.760},\n    \"pythia_temp09\":   {\"acc\": 0.768},\n    \"pythia_topk50\":   {\"acc\": 0.689},\n    \"olmo_nucleus\":    {\"acc\": 0.789},\n    \"olmo_temp09\":     {\"acc\": 0.823},\n    \"olmo_topk50\":     {\"acc\": 0.744},\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\":  {\"acc\": 0.843},\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\":   {\"acc\": 0.859},\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\":   {\"acc\": 0.758},\n}\n\n\nclass TextDataset(Dataset):\n    def __init__(self, texts, tokenizer, max_len=512):\n        self.encodings = tokenizer(\n            texts, max_length=max_len, truncation=True,\n            padding=\"max_length\", return_tensors=\"pt\"\n        )\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return self.encodings[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n        return {k: v[idx] for k, v in self.encodings.items()}\n\n\ndef load_cell_data(data_dir, max_per_depth=5000):\n    texts, labels = [], []\n    for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS):\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\"depth_{d}.jsonl\")\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\n            print(f\"  WARNING: {fpath} not found, skipping depth {d}\")\n            continue\n        count = 0\n        with open(fpath) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                texts.append(rec[\"text\"])\n                labels.append(d)\n                count += 1\n                if count >= max_per_depth:\n                    break\n        print(f\"  depth_{d}: {count} samples\")\n    return texts, np.array(labels)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_log_likelihoods(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n    all_ll = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        token_ll = token_ll * mask\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_ll = token_ll.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_ll.append(mean_ll.cpu().numpy())\n    return np.concatenate(all_ll)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n    all_scores = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\n        discrepancy = token_ll + entropy\n        discrepancy = discrepancy * mask\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_d = (discrepancy * mask).sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        sum_sq = ((discrepancy - mean_d.unsqueeze(1)) ** 2 * mask).sum(dim=1)\n        std_d = (sum_sq / lengths.clamp(min=2)).sqrt().clamp(min=1e-8)\n        score = mean_d / std_d\n        all_scores.append(score.cpu().numpy())\n    return np.concatenate(all_scores)\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc(scores, labels, pair):\n    d_lo, d_hi = pair\n    mask = (labels == d_lo) | (labels == d_hi)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    X = scores[mask].reshape(-1, 1)\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_hi).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n        return None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    aucs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n        rf.fit(X[tr], y[tr])\n        prob = rf.predict_proba(X[te])[:, 1]\n        aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[te], prob))\n    return round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc_combined(bino_scores, fdgpt_scores, labels, pair):\n    d_lo, d_hi = pair\n    mask = (labels == d_lo) | (labels == d_hi)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    X = np.column_stack([bino_scores[mask], fdgpt_scores[mask]])\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_hi).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n        return None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    aucs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n        rf.fit(X[tr], y[tr])\n        prob = rf.predict_proba(X[te])[:, 1]\n        aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[te], prob))\n    return round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef compute_monotonicity(scores, labels):\n    rho, pval = spearmanr(scores, labels)\n    return round(rho, 4), round(pval, 6)\n\n\ndef isotonic_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(scores, labels):\n        iso = IsotonicRegression(y_min=0, y_max=NUM_DEPTHS - 1, out_of_bounds=\"clip\")\n        iso.fit(scores[tr], labels[tr].astype(float))\n        preds = np.round(iso.predict(scores[te])).astype(int)\n        preds = np.clip(preds, 0, NUM_DEPTHS - 1)\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[te], preds))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.std(accs), 4)\n\n\ndef d0_binary_accuracy(scores, labels):\n    binary = (labels >= 1).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(binary)) < 2:\n        return None, None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs, aucs = [], []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(scores, binary):\n        thresh = np.median(scores[tr][labels[tr] == 0])\n        higher_is_gen = np.mean(scores[tr][labels[tr] >= 1]) > np.mean(scores[tr][labels[tr] == 0])\n        if higher_is_gen:\n            preds = (scores[te] > thresh).astype(int)\n        else:\n            preds = (scores[te] < thresh).astype(int)\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(binary[te], preds))\n        if higher_is_gen:\n            aucs.append(roc_auc_score(binary[te], scores[te]))\n        else:\n            aucs.append(roc_auc_score(binary[te], -scores[te]))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef lr_4class_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5):\n    X = scores.reshape(-1, 1) if scores.ndim == 1 else scores\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, labels):\n        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42, multi_class=\"multinomial\")\n        clf.fit(X[tr], labels[tr])\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[te], clf.predict(X[te])))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.std(accs), 4)\n\n\ndef analyze_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf, tok_obs, tok_perf,\n                 device, batch_size, scores_dir):\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  {cell_name}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    texts, labels = load_cell_data(data_dir)\n    if len(texts) == 0:\n        return None\n    n = len(texts)\n    print(f\"  Total: {n} samples, depths: {np.bincount(labels)}\")\n\n    cache_path = os.path.join(scores_dir, f\"{cell_name}_scores.npz\")\n    if os.path.exists(cache_path):\n        print(f\"  Loading cached scores from {cache_path}\")\n        cached = np.load(cache_path)\n        bino_scores = cached[\"bino\"]\n        fdgpt_scores = cached[\"fdgpt\"]\n    else:\n        t0 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing LL (observer: gpt2-xl)...\")\n        ll_obs = compute_log_likelihoods(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n        t1 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing LL (performer: pythia-1.4b)...\")\n        ll_perf = compute_log_likelihoods(model_perf, tok_perf, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n\n        t2 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing Fast-DetectGPT scores...\")\n        fdgpt_scores = compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t2:.1f}s\")\n\n        bino_scores = (-ll_obs) / np.clip(-ll_perf, 1e-8, None)\n\n        np.savez(cache_path, bino=bino_scores, fdgpt=fdgpt_scores, labels=labels)\n        print(f\"  Scores cached to {cache_path}\")\n\n    result = {\"cell\": cell_name, \"n_samples\": n}\n    pairs = [(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)]\n    pair_names = [\"d0v1\", \"d1v2\", \"d2v3\"]\n\n    # --- Binoculars ---\n    bino_lr_acc, bino_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_iso_acc, bino_iso_std = isotonic_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_rho, bino_rho_p = compute_monotonicity(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_d0_acc, bino_d0_auc = d0_binary_accuracy(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        bino_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc(bino_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"binoculars\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": bino_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": bino_lr_std,\n        \"isotonic_acc\": bino_iso_acc,\n        \"isotonic_std\": bino_iso_std,\n        \"spearman_rho\": bino_rho,\n        \"spearman_p\": bino_rho_p,\n        \"d0_binary_acc\": bino_d0_acc,\n        \"d0_binary_auc\": bino_d0_auc,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": bino_pairwise,\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                      \"std\": round(float(bino_scores[labels == d].std()), 4),\n                      \"median\": round(float(np.median(bino_scores[labels == d])), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # --- Fast-DetectGPT ---\n    fdgpt_lr_acc, fdgpt_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_iso_acc, fdgpt_iso_std = isotonic_cv(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_rho, fdgpt_rho_p = compute_monotonicity(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_d0_acc, fdgpt_d0_auc = d0_binary_accuracy(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        fdgpt_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc(fdgpt_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"fast_detectgpt\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": fdgpt_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": fdgpt_lr_std,\n        \"isotonic_acc\": fdgpt_iso_acc,\n        \"isotonic_std\": fdgpt_iso_std,\n        \"spearman_rho\": fdgpt_rho,\n        \"spearman_p\": fdgpt_rho_p,\n        \"d0_binary_acc\": fdgpt_d0_acc,\n        \"d0_binary_auc\": fdgpt_d0_auc,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": fdgpt_pairwise,\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(fdgpt_scores[labels == d].mean()), 4),\n                      \"std\": round(float(fdgpt_scores[labels == d].std()), 4),\n                      \"median\": round(float(np.median(fdgpt_scores[labels == d])), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # --- Combined (2-feature) ---\n    X_comb = np.column_stack([bino_scores, fdgpt_scores])\n    comb_lr_acc, comb_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(X_comb, labels)\n    comb_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        comb_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc_combined(bino_scores, fdgpt_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"combined\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": comb_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": comb_lr_std,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": comb_pairwise,\n    }\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(f\"\\n  {cell_name} results:\")\n    print(f\"    Binoculars  LR-4cls: {bino_lr_acc:.4f}  Iso: {bino_iso_acc:.4f}  \"\n          f\"rho: {bino_rho:.4f}  d0-bin: {bino_d0_acc}\")\n    print(f\"    FastDetGPT  LR-4cls: {fdgpt_lr_acc:.4f}  Iso: {fdgpt_iso_acc:.4f}  \"\n          f\"rho: {fdgpt_rho:.4f}  d0-bin: {fdgpt_d0_acc}\")\n    print(f\"    Combined    LR-4cls: {comb_lr_acc:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (Bino):  d0v1={bino_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n          f\"d1v2={bino_pairwise['d1v2']}  d2v3={bino_pairwise['d2v3']}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (FDGPT): d0v1={fdgpt_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n          f\"d1v2={fdgpt_pairwise['d1v2']}  d2v3={fdgpt_pairwise['d2v3']}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (Comb):  d0v1={comb_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n          f\"d1v2={comb_pairwise['d1v2']}  d2v3={comb_pairwise['d2v3']}\")\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef print_comparison_table(all_results, cells):\n    print(f\"\\n\\n{'='*110}\")\n    print(\"  COMPARISON TABLE\")\n    print(f\"{'='*110}\")\n\n    header = (f\"  {'Cell':<16} {'Method':<14} {'4cls-Acc':<10} {'d0v1':<8} \"\n              f\"{'d1v2':<8} {'d2v3':<8} {'Spearman':<10} {'d0-bin':<8}\")\n    print(header)\n    print(f\"  {'-'*106}\")\n\n    for cell_name in cells:\n        if cell_name not in all_results:\n            continue\n        r = all_results[cell_name]\n        ref = RF_REFERENCE.get(cell_name, {})\n\n        # RF reference\n        rf_acc = ref.get(\"acc\", \"\")\n        rf_d0v1 = ref.get(\"d0v1\", \"\")\n        rf_d1v2 = ref.get(\"d1v2\", \"\")\n        rf_d2v3 = ref.get(\"d2v3\", \"\")\n        print(f\"  {cell_name:<16} {'RF-19feat':<14} {rf_acc:<10} \"\n              f\"{rf_d0v1:<8} {rf_d1v2:<8} {rf_d2v3:<8} {'-1.0':<10} {'—':<8}\")\n\n        # Binoculars\n        b = r[\"binoculars\"]\n        print(f\"  {'':<16} {'Bino-LR':<14} {b['lr_4class_acc']:<10.4f} \"\n              f\"{b['pairwise_auc']['d0v1']:<8} {b['pairwise_auc']['d1v2']:<8} \"\n              f\"{b['pairwise_auc']['d2v3']:<8} {b['spearman_rho']:<10.4f} \"\n              f\"{b['d0_binary_acc']:<8}\")\n\n        # Fast-DetectGPT\n        f = r[\"fast_detectgpt\"]\n        print(f\"  {'':<16} {'FDGPT-LR':<14} > /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"\nOrdinal depth resolution analysis for Binoculars / Fast-DetectGPT baselines.\n\nExtends distributions\n\nUsage:\n  python scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py --gpu 0 --pilot\n  python scripts/detection_baselines_ordinal.py --gpu 0  # all 9 cells\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.isotonic import IsotonicRegression\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, roc_auc_score\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\n\nGRID_CELLS = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": \"data/\",\n    \"pythia_temp09\": \"data/pythia_temp09/\",\n    \"pythia_topk50\": \"data/pythia_topk50/\",\n    \"olmo_nucleus\": \"data/olmo/\",\n    \"olmo_temp09\": \"data/olmo_temp09/\",\n    \"olmo_topk50\": \"data/olmo_topk50/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\": \"data/gpt2xl/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\": \"data/gpt2xl_temp09/\",\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\": \"data/gpt2xl_topk50/\",\n}\n\nMODEL_PATHS = {\n    \"gpt2xl\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl\",\n    \"pythia\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\",\n}\n\nNUM_DEPTHS = 4\nMAX_SEQ_LEN = 512\nDEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE = 16\n\nRF_REFERENCE = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\":  {\"acc\": 0.787, \"d0v1\": 0.999, \"d1v2\": 0.895, \"d2v3\": 0.760},\n    \"pythia_temp09\":   {\"acc\": 0.768},\n    \"pythia_topk50\":   {\"acc\": 0.689},\n    \"olmo_nucleus\":    {\"acc\": 0.789},\n    \"olmo_temp09\":     {\"acc\": 0.823},\n    \"olmo_topk50\":     {\"acc\": 0.744},\n    \"gpt2xl_nucleus\":  {\"acc\": 0.843},\n    \"gpt2xl_temp09\":   {\"acc\": 0.859},\n    \"gpt2xl_topk50\":   {\"acc\": 0.758},\n}\n\n\nclass TextDataset(Dataset):\n    def __init__(self, texts, tokenizer, max_len=512):\n        self.encodings = tokenizer(\n            texts, max_length=max_len, truncation=True,\n            padding=\"max_length\", return_tensors=\"pt\"\n        )\n\n    def __len__(self):\n        return self.encodings[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\n        return {k: v[idx] for k, v in self.encodings.items()}\n\n\ndef load_cell_data(data_dir, max_per_depth=5000):\n    texts, labels = [], []\n    for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS):\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\"depth_{d}.jsonl\")\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\n            print(f\"  WARNING: {fpath} not found, skipping depth {d}\")\n            continue\n        count = 0\n        with open(fpath) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                texts.append(rec[\"text\"])\n                labels.append(d)\n                count += 1\n                if count >= max_per_depth:\n                    break\n        print(f\"  depth_{d}: {count} samples\")\n    return texts, np.array(labels)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_log_likelihoods(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n    all_ll = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:]\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        token_ll = token_ll * mask\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_ll = token_ll.sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        all_ll.append(mean_ll.cpu().numpy())\n    return np.concatenate(all_ll)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model, tokenizer, texts, device, batch_size):\n    dataset = TextDataset(texts, tokenizer, MAX_SEQ_LEN)\n    loader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False)\n    all_scores = []\n    for batch in loader:\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        attention_mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].to(device)\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\n        logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1, :]\n        targets = input_ids[:, 1:]\n        mask = attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\n        token_ll = log_probs.gather(2, targets.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n        entropy = -(probs * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\n        discrepancy = token_ll + entropy\n        discrepancy = discrepancy * mask\n        lengths = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1)\n        mean_d = (discrepancy * mask).sum(dim=1) / lengths\n        sum_sq = ((discrepancy - mean_d.unsqueeze(1)) ** 2 * mask).sum(dim=1)\n        std_d = (sum_sq / lengths.clamp(min=2)).sqrt().clamp(min=1e-8)\n        score = mean_d / std_d\n        all_scores.append(score.cpu().numpy())\n    return np.concatenate(all_scores)\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc(scores, labels, pair):\n    d_lo, d_hi = pair\n    mask = (labels == d_lo) | (labels == d_hi)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    X = scores[mask].reshape(-1, 1)\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_hi).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n        return None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    aucs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n        rf.fit(X[tr], y[tr])\n        prob = rf.predict_proba(X[te])[:, 1]\n        aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[te], prob))\n    return round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc_combined(bino_scores, fdgpt_scores, labels, pair):\n    d_lo, d_hi = pair\n    mask = (labels == d_lo) | (labels == d_hi)\n    if mask.sum() < 10:\n        return None\n    X = np.column_stack([bino_scores[mask], fdgpt_scores[mask]])\n    y = (labels[mask] == d_hi).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n        return None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    aucs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, y):\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)\n        rf.fit(X[tr], y[tr])\n        prob = rf.predict_proba(X[te])[:, 1]\n        aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[te], prob))\n    return round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef compute_monotonicity(scores, labels):\n    rho, pval = spearmanr(scores, labels)\n    return round(rho, 4), round(pval, 6)\n\n\ndef isotonic_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(scores, labels):\n        iso = IsotonicRegression(y_min=0, y_max=NUM_DEPTHS - 1, out_of_bounds=\"clip\")\n        iso.fit(scores[tr], labels[tr].astype(float))\n        preds = np.round(iso.predict(scores[te])).astype(int)\n        preds = np.clip(preds, 0, NUM_DEPTHS - 1)\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[te], preds))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.std(accs), 4)\n\n\ndef d0_binary_accuracy(scores, labels):\n    binary = (labels >= 1).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(binary)) < 2:\n        return None, None\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs, aucs = [], []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(scores, binary):\n        thresh = np.median(scores[tr][labels[tr] == 0])\n        higher_is_gen = np.mean(scores[tr][labels[tr] >= 1]) > np.mean(scores[tr][labels[tr] == 0])\n        if higher_is_gen:\n            preds = (scores[te] > thresh).astype(int)\n        else:\n            preds = (scores[te] < thresh).astype(int)\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(binary[te], preds))\n        if higher_is_gen:\n            aucs.append(roc_auc_score(binary[te], scores[te]))\n        else:\n            aucs.append(roc_auc_score(binary[te], -scores[te]))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.mean(aucs), 4)\n\n\ndef lr_4class_cv(scores, labels, n_splits=5):\n    X = scores.reshape(-1, 1) if scores.ndim == 1 else scores\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    accs = []\n    for tr, te in skf.split(X, labels):\n        clf = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42, multi_class=\"multinomial\")\n        clf.fit(X[tr], labels[tr])\n        accs.append(accuracy_score(labels[te], clf.predict(X[te])))\n    return round(np.mean(accs), 4), round(np.std(accs), 4)\n\n\ndef analyze_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf, tok_obs, tok_perf,\n                 device, batch_size, scores_dir):\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  {cell_name}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    texts, labels = load_cell_data(data_dir)\n    if len(texts) == 0:\n        return None\n    n = len(texts)\n    print(f\"  Total: {n} samples, depths: {np.bincount(labels)}\")\n\n    cache_path = os.path.join(scores_dir, f\"{cell_name}_scores.npz\")\n    if os.path.exists(cache_path):\n        print(f\"  Loading cached scores from {cache_path}\")\n        cached = np.load(cache_path)\n        bino_scores = cached[\"bino\"]\n        fdgpt_scores = cached[\"fdgpt\"]\n    else:\n        t0 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing LL (observer: gpt2-xl)...\")\n        ll_obs = compute_log_likelihoods(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n        t1 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing LL (performer: pythia-1.4b)...\")\n        ll_perf = compute_log_likelihoods(model_perf, tok_perf, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t1:.1f}s\")\n\n        t2 = time.time()\n        print(\"  Computing Fast-DetectGPT scores...\")\n        fdgpt_scores = compute_fast_detectgpt_scores(model_obs, tok_obs, texts, device, batch_size)\n        print(f\"    {time.time()-t2:.1f}s\")\n\n        bino_scores = (-ll_obs) / np.clip(-ll_perf, 1e-8, None)\n\n        np.savez(cache_path, bino=bino_scores, fdgpt=fdgpt_scores, labels=labels)\n        print(f\"  Scores cached to {cache_path}\")\n\n    result = {\"cell\": cell_name, \"n_samples\": n}\n    pairs = [(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)]\n    pair_names = [\"d0v1\", \"d1v2\", \"d2v3\"]\n\n    # --- Binoculars ---\n    bino_lr_acc, bino_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_iso_acc, bino_iso_std = isotonic_cv(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_rho, bino_rho_p = compute_monotonicity(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_d0_acc, bino_d0_auc = d0_binary_accuracy(bino_scores, labels)\n    bino_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        bino_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc(bino_scores, labels, pair)\n\n    result[\"binoculars\"] = {\n        \"lr_4class_acc\": bino_lr_acc,\n        \"lr_4class_std\": bino_lr_std,\n        \"isotonic_acc\": bino_iso_acc,\n        \"isotonic_std\": bino_iso_std,\n        \"spearman_rho\": bino_rho,\n        \"spearman_p\": bino_rho_p,\n        \"d0_binary_acc\": bino_d0_acc,\n        \"d0_binary_auc\": bino_d0_auc,\n        \"pairwise_auc\": bino_pairwise,\n        \"score_stats\": {\n            f\"d{d}\": {\"mean\": round(float(np.median(bino_scores[labels == d])), 4)}\n            for d in range(NUM_DEPTHS)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # --- Fast-DetectGPT ---\n    fdgpt_lr_acc, fdgpt_lr_std = fdgpt_d0_auc = d0_binary_accuracy(fdgpt_scores, labels)\n    fdgpt_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, pair in zip(pair_names, pairs):\n        fdgpt_pairwise[pname] = pairwise_auc(fdgpt_scores, labels, }\n\n    # --- Combined (2-feature) ---\n    X_comb = np.column_stack([bino_scores, fdgpt_scores])\n    comb_lr_acc, comb_lr_std = lr_4class_cv(X_comb, labels)\n    comb_pairwise = {}\n    for pname, \"\n          f\"rho: {fdgpt_rho:.4f}  d0-bin: {fdgpt_d0_acc}\")\n    print(f\"    Combined    LR-4cls: {comb_lr_acc:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"    Pairwise AUC (Bino):  d0v1={bino_pairwise['d0v1']}  \"\n if bino_accs:\n        print(f\"\\n  Mean 4-class Acc:  RF={np.mean(rf_accs):.4f}  \"\n              f\"Bino={np.mean(bino_accs):.4f}  FDGPT={np.mean(fdgpt_accs):.4f}  \"\n              f\"Combined={np.mean(comb_accs):.4f}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--pilot\", args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    scores_dir = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"scores\")\n    os.makedirs(scores_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    print(\"Loading models...\")\n    t0 = time.time()\n    tok_obs = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATHS[\"gpt2xl\"])\n    tok_obs.pad_token = tok_obs.eos_token\n    model_obs = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATHS[\"gpt2xl\"], torch_dtype=torch.float16\n    ).to(device).eval()\n    print(f\"  gpt2-xl loaded ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)\")\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n    tok_perf = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATHS[\"pythia\"])\n    tok_perf.pad_token = tok_perf.eos_token\n    model_perf = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATHS[\"pythia\"], torch_dtype=torch.float16\n    ).to(device).eval()\n    print(f\"  pythia-1.4b loaded ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)\")\n\n    cells = {\"pythia_nucleus\": GRID_CELLS[\"pythia_nucleus\"]} if args.pilot else GRID_CELLS\n    all_results = {}\n    start = time.time()\n\n    for cell_name, data_dir in cells.items():\n        r = analyze_cell(cell_name, data_dir, model_obs, model_perf,\n                         tok_obs, tok_perf, device, args.batch_size, scores_dir)\n        if r:\n            all_results[cell_name] = r\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - start\n    print_comparison_table(all_results, cells)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"experiment\": \"detection_baselines_ordinal\",\n        \"plan_id\": \"plan_018\",\n        \"per_cell\": all_results,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"pilot_mode\": args.pilot,\n    }\n\n    out_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"results.json\")\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSaved to {out_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n With gpt2's tokenizer and max_length=512, that's fine for memory. But for the RTX PRO 6000, gpt2-xl inference with batch_size=16, seq_len=512 should be fast. \n\nLet me think about the actual compute time:\n- gpt2-xl has 1558M parameters\n\n\n- Forward pass FLOPs per token ≈ 2 * parameters = 3.1 GFLOPs\n- Batch of 16 * 512 = 8192 tokens → 25.4 TFLOPs\n- RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell has ~209 TFLOPS (FP16) \n- Time per batch ≈ 25.4 / 209 ≈ 0.12s\n- 1250 batches → 150s per pass\n- 3 passes → 450s = 7.5 minutes\n- Plus tokenization overhead\n\nSo I'm working through the math on how long this should take.
